\mathfrak{E}\subseteq\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2},\gamma_{3}\}
and define

\displaystyle C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)

\displaystyle:=\left\{\phi\in C^{\infty}(\bar{T}):\bigcup_{\gamma\in\mathfrak{E}}\gamma\cap\supp\phi=\emptyset\right\}.

For
1<p<\infty
and
0\leq s<\infty
, the space
C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
is dense in
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
.

Proof.

Let
\mathfrak{E}\subseteq\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2},\gamma_{3}\}
and
1<p<\infty
be given.

Step 1:
0\leq s<1/p
.
The space
C^{\infty}_{c}(T)\subseteq C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
is dense in
W^{s,p}(T)=W_{\mathfrak{E}}^{s,p}(T)
(see e.g.
[
37
, Theorem 1.4.5.2]
).

Step 2:
s\geq 1/p
and
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1}\}
.
Let
s=m+\sigma
with
m\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
and
\sigma\in[0,1)
, and let
f\in W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
. For
n\in\mathbb{N}
, we construct a partition of unity on
T
as follows. Let
\{\bm{a}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{3}
denote the vertices of
T
labeled counterclockwise as in
fig.

1(b)
and define the following sets:

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{0}

\displaystyle:=\left\{\bm{x}\in T:\dist(\bm{x},\partial T)>\frac{1}{2n}\right\},\qquad

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{(i-1)(n-1)+j}=\mathcal{U}_{j}^{(i)}

\displaystyle:=B\left(\bm{a}_{i+2}+\frac{j}{n}\mathbf{t}_{i},\frac{3}{4n}\right)\cap\bar{T},\qquad

\displaystyle 1\leq j\leq n-1,\ 1\leq i\leq 3,

\displaystyle\mathcal{U}_{3n-3+k}

\displaystyle:=B\left(\bm{a}_{k},\frac{3}{4n}\right)\cap\bar{T},\qquad

\displaystyle 1\leq k\leq 3,

where we use the notation
B(\bm{x},r)
to denote the ball of radius
r
centered at
\bm{x}
. By construction,
T\subset\bigcup_{i=0}^{3n}\mathcal{U}_{i}
, and so there exists a partition of unity
\{\phi_{i}\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\mathcal{U}_{i}):0\leq i\leq 3n\}
satisfying

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{3n}\phi_{i}=1\quad\text{and}\quad\|D^{k}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{U}_{i}}\lesssim_{k}n^{k},\qquad 0\leq i\leq 3n,\ \forall k\in\mathbb{N}_{0}.

We denote
f_{i}:=\phi_{i}f
for
0\leq i\leq 3n-3
and set
\mathcal{V}_{i}:=\mathcal{U}_{i}\cap T
for
0\leq i\leq 3n
.

Let
\{\delta_{i}\}_{i=0}^{3n-3}
be arbitrary positive constants. The construction proceeds in several parts.

Part (a).
The function
f_{0}:=\phi_{0}f
satisfies

\displaystyle D^{l}f_{0}|_{\partial\mathcal{U}_{0}}

\displaystyle=0,\qquad

\displaystyle 0\leq l<s-\frac{1}{p},

\displaystyle\|\dist(\cdot,\partial\mathcal{U}_{0})^{-\sigma}D^{m}f_{0}\|_{p,\mathcal{U}_{0}}\lesssim_{m,p}n^{\frac{1}{p}+m}\|f\|_{m,p,\mathcal{U}_{0}}

\displaystyle<\infty\qquad

\displaystyle\text{if $\sigma p=1$}.

By
[
37
, Theorem 1.4.5.2]
, there exists a
\psi_{0}\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\mathcal{U}_{0})
satisfying

\displaystyle\delta_{0}\geq\|f_{0}-\psi_{0}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{U}_{0}}+\begin{cases}\|\dist(\cdot,\partial\mathcal{U}_{0})^{-\sigma}D^{m}(f_{0}-\psi_{0})\|_{p,\mathcal{U}_{0}}&\text{if }\sigma p=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Since
f\in W^{s,p}(T)
, we apply the same argument to
f_{i}:=\phi_{i}f
on
\mathcal{V}_{i}
,
1\leq i\leq n-1
, to show that there exists a
\psi_{i}\in C^{\infty}_{c}(\mathcal{V}_{i})
satisfying

\displaystyle\delta_{i}\geq\|f_{i}-\psi_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\begin{cases}\|\dist(\cdot,\partial\mathcal{V}_{i})^{-\sigma}D^{m}(f_{i}-\psi_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}&\text{if }\sigma p=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Part (b).
For
n\leq i\leq 3n-2
,
f\in W^{s,p}(\mathcal{V}_{i})
, and so there exists a
\rho_{i}\in C^{\infty}(\bar{\mathcal{V}}_{i})
satisfying
\|f-\rho_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\leq\delta_{i}n^{m+2}
thanks to
[
37
, Theorem 1.4.5.2]
. Then, the function
\psi_{i}:=\phi_{i}\rho_{i}
satisfies

\displaystyle\|f_{i}-\psi_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\|\phi_{i}\|_{m,\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\|f-\rho_{i}\|_{m,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\sum_{l=0}^{m}\|D^{l}\phi_{i}D^{m-l}(f-\rho_{i})\|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{s,p}\delta_{i},

where we used
[
43
, Theorem 6.3]
to conclude that

\displaystyle\|D^{l}\phi_{i}D^{m-l}(f-\rho_{i})\|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\|D^{l}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}^{1-\sigma}\|D^{l+1}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}^{\sigma}\|D^{m-l}(f-\rho_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\|D^{l}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\|D^{m-l}(f-\rho_{i})\|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta_{i}.

Moreover, when
\sigma p=1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}(f_{i}-\psi_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{p}n^{\sigma}\|D^{m}(f_{i}-\psi_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{s,p}\delta_{i}.

Part (c).
For
3n-1\leq i\leq 3n
, we will show that

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}=0\quad\text{and if $\sigma p=1$,}\quad\lim_{n\to\infty}\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}f_{i}\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}=0.

(110)

Thanks to
[
43
, Theorem 6.3]
, there holds

\displaystyle\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sum_{l=0}^{j}\|D^{l}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\|D^{j-l}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\sum_{l=0}^{m}\|D^{l}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\|D^{m-l}f\|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{l=0}^{m}\|D^{l}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}^{1-\sigma}\|D^{l+1}\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,\mathcal{V}_{i}}^{\sigma}\|D^{m-l}f\|_{p,\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sum_{l=0}^{j}n^{l}\|D^{j-l}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\sum_{l=0}^{m}\left(n^{l+\sigma}\|D^{m-l}f\|_{p,\mathcal{U}_{i}}+n^{l}\|D^{m-l}f\|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\right).

Similar computations show that for
\sigma p=1
, there holds

\displaystyle\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}f_{i}\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{s,p}\sum_{j=0}^{m}n^{j}\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m-j}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}.

Since
\mathcal{V}_{i}\cap\gamma_{1}\neq\emptyset
, Poincaré’s inequality gives

\displaystyle\|D^{r}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{r,p}n^{-(s-r)}|D^{m}f|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\qquad 0\leq r\leq m,

and so
\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{s,p}|D^{m}f|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}
. Moreover, if
\sigma p=1
, then
D^{m-j}f\in W_{\mathfrak{E}}^{1,p}(\mathcal{V}_{i})
for
1\leq j\leq m
, and so
[
23
, Theorem 5.2]
,
[
34
, Theorem 3.2]
, and a standard scaling argument give

\displaystyle\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m-j}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim n^{\sigma-1}\|\omega_{1}^{-1}D^{m-j}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p}n^{\sigma-1}\|D^{m-j+1}f\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}n^{-j}|D^{m}f|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}},

and so
\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}f_{i}\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\lesssim_{s,p}|D^{m}f|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}
. Equality
eq.

110
now follows from that fact that
|D^{m}f|_{\sigma,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
since
|\mathcal{V}_{i}|\to 0
as
n\to\infty
.

Part (d).
Let
\epsilon>0
be given. First, choose
n
large enough so that

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{2}\geq\sum_{i=3n-1}^{3n}\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\begin{cases}\sum_{i=3n-1}^{3n}\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{k}f_{i}\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}&\text{if }\sigma p=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Then, for
\{\delta_{i}\}_{i=0}^{3n-2}
chosen sufficiently small, we construct
\psi_{i}
as above so that

\displaystyle\frac{\epsilon}{2}\geq\sum_{i=0}^{3n-2}\|f_{i}-\psi_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\begin{cases}\sum_{i=3n-1}^{3n}\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}(f_{i}-\psi_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}&\text{if }\sigma p=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Let
\tilde{\psi}_{i}
denote the extension of
\psi_{i}
by zero to
T\setminus\mathcal{U}_{i}
,
0\leq i\leq 3n-2
and set
\tilde{\psi}_{j}\equiv 0
for
3n-1\leq j\leq 3n
. Then,
\tilde{\psi}_{i}\in C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
, and the function
\psi=\sum_{i=0}^{3n}\tilde{\psi}_{i}
then satisfies
\psi\in C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|f-\psi\right\|^{{\kern-24.11778pt\kern 24.50667pt{}}}_{{\kern-27.9525pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 24.50667pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|f-\psi\right\|^{{\kern-24.11778pt\kern 24.50667pt{}}}_{{\kern-27.9525pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 24.50667pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|f-\psi\right\|^{{\kern-12.26265pt\kern 12.65154pt{}}}_{{\kern-15.14459pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 12.65154pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|f-\psi\right\|^{{\kern-8.64792pt\kern 9.0368pt{}}}_{{\kern-11.52986pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 9.0368pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{i=0}^{3n}\|f_{i}-\psi_{i}\|_{s,p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}+\begin{cases}\sum_{i=0}^{3n}\|\omega_{1}^{-\sigma}D^{m}(f_{i}-\psi_{i})\|_{p,\mathcal{V}_{i}}&\text{if }\sigma p=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise},\end{cases}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\epsilon,

which shows that
C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
is dense in
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
.

Step 3:
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{2}\}
or
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{3}\}
.
If
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{2}\}
, the density of
C^{\infty}_{\gamma_{2}}(T)
in
W_{\gamma_{2}}^{s,p}(T)
follows from the fact that
f\in W_{\gamma_{2}}^{s,p}(T)
if and only if
f\circ\mathfrak{F}_{2}^{-1}\in W_{\gamma_{1}}^{s,p}(T)
, where
\mathfrak{F}_{2}^{-1}(x_{1},x_{2})=(1-x_{1}-x_{2},x_{1})
is the inverse of
\mathfrak{F}_{2}
defined in
eq.

96
. The case
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{3}\}
follows from similar arguments using the mapping
\mathfrak{F}_{2}
.

Step 4:
|\mathfrak{E}|=2
.
Now let
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}
. The density of
C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
in
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
may be shown using a similar construction to the case
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1}\}
. In particular, we apply the construction of Step 1 Part (a) for
0\leq i\leq 2n-2
and
i=3n
, Part (b) for
2n-1\leq i\leq 3n-3
, Part (c) for
3n-2\leq i\leq 3n-1
, and proceed analogously as in Part (d). The remaining cases for
|\mathfrak{E}|=2
are proved along similar lines.

Step 5:
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2},\gamma_{3}\}
.
This case is a restatement of
[
37
, Lemma 1.4.5.2]
.

A.2
Hardy inequalities

First, we construct a bounded averaging operator.

Lemma 40
.

There exists a linear operator
\mathcal{H}_{1}
satisfying the following properties:

(i)

\mathcal{H}_{1}
maps
C(\bar{T})
boundedly into
C(\bar{T})
, and there holds

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)(\bm{x})=\frac{1}{x_{1}}\int_{0}^{x_{1}}f(u,x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}{u}=\int_{0}^{1}f(ux_{1},x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}{u}\qquad\forall\bm{x}\in T.

(111)

(ii)

\mathcal{H}_{1}
maps
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
boundedly into
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
and for all
p\in(1,\infty)
,
s\in[0,\infty)
,
r\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, and
\mathfrak{E}\in\{\emptyset,\{\gamma_{1}\},\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}\}
. In particular,

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-35.69415pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-35.69415pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-17.48471pt\kern 17.8736pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.01514pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 17.8736pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-13.03197pt\kern 13.42085pt{}}}_{{\kern-18.5624pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 13.42085pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p,r}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.03227pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.03227pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-12.96793pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-10.73183pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}\qquad

\displaystyle\forall f\in W_{\mathfrak{E},r}^{s,p}(T).

(112)

Proof.

Step 1: Continuity on
C(\bar{T})
.
Let
f\in C(\bar{T})
and define
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)
by
eq.

111
. Elementary arguments show that
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in C(\bar{T})
with
\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(\phi_{i})\|_{\infty,T}\leq\|\phi_{i}\|_{\infty,T}
.

Step 2: Extension to
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
when
\mathfrak{E}=\emptyset
.
Let
f\in C^{\infty}(\bar{T})
and
1<p<\infty
. For
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{2}
, there holds

\displaystyle D^{\alpha}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\int_{0}^{1}u^{\alpha_{1}}(D^{\alpha}f)(ux_{1},x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}{u}=\frac{1}{x_{1}^{\alpha_{1}+1}}\int_{0}^{x_{1}}u^{\alpha_{1}}(D^{\alpha}f)(u,x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}{u},

(113)

and so
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in C^{\infty}(\bar{T})
. Moreover, Hardy’s inequality
[
39
, Theorem 327]
gives

\displaystyle\|D^{\alpha}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\|_{p,T}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1-x_{2}}\left|\frac{1}{x_{1}}\int_{0}^{x_{1}}|D^{\alpha}f(u,x_{2})|\,\mathrm{d}{u}\right|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{1}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{p}{p-1}\right)^{p}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1-x_{2}}|D^{\alpha}f(x_{1},x_{2})|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{1}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{2}}.

Consequently, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\|_{s,p,T}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\|f\|_{s,p,T}\qquad\forall f\in C^{\infty}(\bar{T}),\ s\in\mathbb{N}_{0}.

Since
C^{\infty}(\bar{T})
is dense in
W^{s,p}(T)

[
37
, Theorem 1.4.5.2]
,
\mathcal{H}_{1}
can be continuously extended to a linear operator from
W^{s,p}(T)
into
W^{s,p}(T)
for
s\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
. The case for non-integer
s\in(0,\infty)
follows from interpolation.

Step 3: Inequality
eq.

112
when
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1}\}
.
The case
r=0
follows from Step 2, so let
r\in\mathbb{N}
. Assume first the
s\leq r
and let
f\in C^{\infty}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
.
Equation

113
shows that
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in C^{\infty}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
. Moreover, for
s=m+1/p
,
m\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, we apply Hardy’s inequality
[
39
, Theorem 327]
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\omega_{1}^{-\frac{1}{p}}D^{\alpha}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\|_{p,T}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{1-x_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{x_{1}}\int_{0}^{x_{1}}\frac{1}{u^{\frac{1}{p}}}|D^{\alpha}f(u,x_{2})|\,\mathrm{d}{u}\right)^{p}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{1}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{p}{p-1}\right)^{p}\|\omega_{1}^{-\frac{1}{p}}D^{\alpha}f\|_{p,T}^{p}

(114)

for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
with
|\alpha|=m
. Thus,
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.4384pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.4384pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-17.48471pt\kern 17.8736pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.04677pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 17.8736pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-13.03197pt\kern 13.42085pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.59402pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 13.42085pt{s,p,T}}}}\lesssim_{s,p}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.77652pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.77652pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-11.99956pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-9.76346pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}
for all
f\in C^{\infty}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
. By density (
lemma

39
),
\mathcal{H}_{1}
maps
W_{\gamma_{1},r}^{s,p}(T)
boundedly into
W_{\gamma_{1},r}^{s,p}(T)
for all
p\in(1,\infty)
and
s\in[0,r]
.

Now let
s>r
and
f\in W_{\gamma_{1},r}^{s,p}(T)
. Step 2 and the arguments above show that
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in W^{s,p}(T)\cap W^{r,p}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
, and so
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{1},r}(T)
if
s-1/p\notin\mathbb{Z}
with
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-37.1463pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-37.1463pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-17.48471pt\kern 17.8736pt{}}}_{{\kern-24.69525pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 17.8736pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-13.03197pt\kern 13.42085pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.24251pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 13.42085pt{s,p,T}}}}\lesssim_{s,p}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-21.48442pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-21.48442pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-14.64804pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-12.41194pt{\gamma_{1},r}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}
. Now let
s=m+1/p
for some
m\in\mathbb{N}
. Then,
f\in C(\bar{T})
and thanks to Step 1 and
eq.

114
, we have

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{1}^{-\frac{1}{p}}\frac{\partial^{m-r-1}D^{r-1}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)}{\partial x_{2}^{m-r-1}}\right\|_{p,T}\lesssim_{p}\left\|\omega_{1}^{-\frac{1}{p}}\frac{\partial^{m-r-1}D^{r-1}f}{\partial x_{2}^{m-r-1}}\right\|_{p,T}.

Consequently,
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{1},r}(T)
and
eq.

112
holds when
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1}\}
.

Step 4: Inequality
eq.

112
when
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}
.
Again let
r\in\mathbb{N}
. Assume first that
s\leq r
. As above,
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
for any
f\in C^{\infty}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
by
eq.

113
, and for
s=m+1/p
,
m\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
, Hardy’s inequality
[
39
, Theorem 327]
gives

\displaystyle\|\omega_{2}^{-\frac{1}{p}}D^{\alpha}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\|_{p,T}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\frac{1}{x_{2}}\int_{0}^{1-x_{2}}\left(\frac{1}{x_{1}}\int_{0}^{x_{1}}|D^{\alpha}f(u,x_{2})|\,\mathrm{d}{u}\right)^{p}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{1}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{p}{p-1}\right)^{p}\|\omega_{2}^{-\frac{1}{p}}D^{\alpha}f\|_{p,T}^{p}

(115)

for all
\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
with
|\alpha|=m
. Inequality
eq.

112
now follows from Step 2 and
eq.

30
. By density,
\mathcal{H}_{1}
maps
W_{\mathfrak{E},r}^{s,p}(T)
boundedly into
W_{\mathfrak{E},r}^{s,p}(K)
for all
p\in(1,\infty)
and
s\in[0,r]
.

Now let
s>r
and
f\in W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
. Arguing analogously as in Step 3, we have
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
if
s-\frac{1}{p}\notin\mathbb{Z}
with
\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-35.69415pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-28.15154pt\kern 28.54042pt{}}}_{{\kern-35.69415pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 28.54042pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-17.48471pt\kern 17.8736pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.01514pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 17.8736pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\right\|^{{\kern-13.03197pt\kern 13.42085pt{}}}_{{\kern-18.5624pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 13.42085pt{s,p,T}}}}\lesssim_{s,p}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.03227pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.03227pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-12.96793pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E},r}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-10.73183pt{\mathfrak{E},r}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}
. Moreover,
eq.

115
gives

\displaystyle\left\|\omega_{2}^{-\frac{1}{p}}\frac{\partial^{m-r-1}D^{r-1}\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)}{\partial x_{1}^{m-r-1}}\right\|_{p,T}\lesssim_{p}\left\|\omega_{2}^{-\frac{1}{p}}\frac{\partial^{m-r-1}D^{r-1}f}{\partial x_{1}^{m-r-1}}\right\|_{p,T}.

Consequently,
\mathcal{H}_{1}(f)\in W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E},r}(T)
and
eq.

112
holds when
\mathfrak{E}=\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}
.

Finally, we state and prove various versions of Hardy’s inequality.

Theorem 41
.

Let
1<p<\infty
and
\emptyset\neq\mathfrak{E}\subseteq\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}
. For
0\leq s<\infty
and
i\in\{1,2\}
such that
\gamma_{i}\in\mathfrak{E}
, the mapping
f\mapsto\omega_{i}^{-1}f
is bounded (i)
W^{s+1,p}(T)\cap W^{1,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
to
W^{s,p}(T)
, and (ii)
W^{s+1,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
to
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
, and there holds

\displaystyle\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\|_{s,p,T}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|_{s,p,T}\qquad

\displaystyle\forall f\in W^{s+1,p}(T)\cap W_{\mathfrak{E}}^{1,p}(T),\qquad

(116)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-25.94547pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-25.94547pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-13.90417pt\kern 14.29306pt{}}}_{{\kern-16.78612pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 14.29306pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-10.79665pt\kern 11.18553pt{}}}_{{\kern-13.67859pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 11.18553pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.78296pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.78296pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-11.74472pt\kern 12.1336pt{}}}_{{\kern-14.62666pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 12.1336pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\mathfrak{E}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-8.76535pt\kern 9.15424pt{}}}_{{\kern-11.6473pt{\mathfrak{E}}\kern 9.15424pt{s,p,T}}}}\qquad

\displaystyle\forall f\in W_{\mathfrak{E}}^{s+1,p}(T).\qquad

(117)

Additionally, for
0\leq s<1
and
i\in\{1,2\}
, the mapping
f\mapsto\omega_{i}^{-s}f
is bounded
W_{\gamma_{i}}^{s,p}(T)
to
L^{p}(T)
, and there holds

\displaystyle\|\omega_{i}^{-s}f\|_{p,T}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.23232pt{\gamma_{i}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.23232pt{\gamma_{i}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-11.45535pt{\gamma_{i}}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-9.21925pt{\gamma_{i}}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}\qquad\forall f\in W_{\gamma_{i}}^{s,p}(T).

(118)

Proof.

Let
1<p<\infty
be given.

Step 1: Inequalities
eqs.

116
and
117
when
\mathfrak{E}\in\{\{\gamma_{1}\},\{\gamma_{1},\gamma_{2}\}\}
.
Thanks to the fundamental theorem of calculus, there holds

\displaystyle f(\bm{x})=\int_{0}^{x_{1}}(\partial_{1}f)(u,x_{2})\,\mathrm{d}{u}=x_{1}\mathcal{H}_{1}(\partial_{1}f)\qquad\forall\bm{x}\in T,\ \forall f\in C^{\infty}_{\gamma_{1}}(T).

(119)

By density (
lemma

39
),
eq.

119
holds for a.e.
\bm{x}\in T
for all
f\in W_{\mathfrak{E}}^{1,p}(T)
.
Lemma

40
and
eq.

119
then show that the mapping
f\mapsto\omega_{i}^{-1}f
is bounded (i) from
W^{s+1,p}(T)\cap W^{1,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
to
W^{s,p}(T)
, and (ii) from
W^{s+1,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
to
W^{s,p}_{\mathfrak{E}}(T)
provided that
\gamma_{i}\in\mathfrak{E}
. Inequalities
eqs.

116
and
117
now follow from
eqs.

112
and
119
.

Step 2: Inequalities
eqs.

116
and
117
when
\mathfrak{E}=\{2\}
.
Note that
f\in W^{s+1,p}(T)\cap W^{1,p}_{\gamma_{2}}(T)
if and only if
g:=f\circ\mathfrak{F}_{1}\in W^{s+1,p}(T)\cap W^{1,p}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
and
f\in W^{s+1}_{\gamma_{2}}(T)
if and only if
g\in W^{s+1}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
, where
\mathfrak{F}_{1}
is defined in
eq.

95
. Inequalities
eqs.

116
and
117
then follow from Step 1.

Step 3: Inequality
eq.

118
with
i=1
.
Now let
0\leq s<1
. For
sp=1
,
eq.

118
follows immediately from the definition of the norm. In the case
sp<1
, the proof of Theorem 1.4.4.4 in
[
37
]
gives

\displaystyle\|\omega_{i}^{-s}f\|_{p,T}\leq\|\dist(\cdot,\partial T)^{-s}f\|_{p,T}\lesssim_{s,p}\|f\|_{s,p,T}=\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.77652pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-17.77652pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 12.87854pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-11.99956pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 7.82639pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{1}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-9.76346pt{\gamma_{1}}\kern 5.59029pt{s,p,T}}}}\qquad\forall f\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{1}}(T).

Finally, let
sp>1
and let
f\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
be given. We denote by
\tilde{f}\in W^{s,p}(T)
any extension of
f
to
\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying
\|\tilde{f}\|_{s,p,\mathbb{R}^{2}}\lesssim_{s,p}\|f\|_{s,p,T}
(see e.g.
[
33
]
or
[
43
, Theorem 8.4]
). Thanks to Theorem 6.79, inequality (6.58), and Remark 6.80 of
[
43
]
, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{\infty}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{|\tilde{f}(x_{1},x_{2})-\tilde{f}(0,x_{2})|^{p}}{x_{1}^{sp}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{2}}\,\mathrm{d}{x_{1}}\lesssim_{s,p}|\tilde{f}|_{s,p,\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}}^{p}.

Since
f|_{\gamma_{1}}=\tilde{f}|_{\gamma_{1}}=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\omega_{1}^{-s}f\|_{p,T}=\|\omega_{1}^{-s}(\tilde{f}-\tilde{f}(0,\cdot))\|_{p,T}\leq\|\omega_{1}^{-s}(\tilde{f}-\tilde{f}(0,\cdot))\|_{p,\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}}\lesssim_{s,p}|\tilde{f}|_{s,p,\mathbb{R}_{+}\times\mathbb{R}},

and so
\|\omega_{1}^{-s}f\|_{p,T}\lesssim_{s,p}\|f\|_{s,p,T}
, which completes the proof of
eq.

118
for
i=1
.

Step 4: Inequality
eq.

118
with
i=2
.
This can be reduced to the case
i=1
using similar arguments as in Step 2 using the mapping
\mathfrak{F}_{1}
defined in
eq.

95
.

Corollary 42
.

Let
1<p<\infty
,
0\leq s<\infty
,
1\leq i\leq 2
, and
r_{1},r_{2}\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
with
r_{i}\geq 1
. Then, for all
f\in W^{s+1,p}_{\gamma_{1},r_{1}}(T)\cap W^{s+1,p}_{\gamma_{2},r_{2}}(T)
, there holds
\omega_{i}^{-1}f\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}(T)\cap W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}(T)
and

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-36.35043pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-36.35043pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-13.90417pt\kern 14.29306pt{}}}_{{\kern-25.19296pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 14.29306pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-10.79665pt\kern 11.18553pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.08543pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 11.18553pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p,r_{1},r_{2}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.18793pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.18793pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-11.74472pt\kern 12.1336pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.03351pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 12.1336pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-8.76535pt\kern 9.15424pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.05414pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}-1}\kern 9.15424pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.64601pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}\kern 12.87854pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-22.64601pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}\kern 12.87854pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-15.80963pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}\kern 7.82639pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-13.57353pt{\gamma_{i},r_{i}}\kern 5.59029pt{s+1,p,T}}}},

(120a)

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.13884pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-22.11075pt\kern 22.49963pt{}}}_{{\kern-33.13884pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 22.49963pt{s,p}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-13.90417pt\kern 14.29306pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.14804pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 14.29306pt{s,p}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\omega_{i}^{-1}f\right\|^{{\kern-10.79665pt\kern 11.18553pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.04051pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 11.18553pt{s,p}}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{s,p,r_{1},r_{2}}\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-29.97633pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-18.94824pt\kern 19.33713pt{}}}_{{\kern-29.97633pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 19.33713pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-11.74472pt\kern 12.1336pt{}}}_{{\kern-20.98859pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 12.1336pt{s,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|\partial_{i}f\right\|^{{\kern-8.76535pt\kern 9.15424pt{}}}_{{\kern-18.00922pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 9.15424pt{s,p,T}}}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathchoice{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.51775pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 12.87854pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-12.48965pt\kern 12.87854pt{}}}_{{\kern-23.51775pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 12.87854pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-7.4375pt\kern 7.82639pt{}}}_{{\kern-16.68137pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 7.82639pt{s+1,p,T}}}}{\hphantom{{}_{{{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}}}}\left\|f\right\|^{{\kern-5.2014pt\kern 5.59029pt{}}}_{{\kern-14.44527pt{\gamma_{j},r_{j}}\kern 5.59029pt{s+1,p,T}}}},

(120b)

where
1\leq j\leq 2
,
j\neq i
.

Proof.

Let
f\in W^{s+1,p}_{\gamma_{1},r_{1}+1}(T)\cap W^{s+1,p}_{\gamma_{2},r_{2}}(T)
be given. By definition, there holds
\partial_{1}f\in W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{1},r_{1}}(T)\cap W^{s,p}_{\gamma_{2},r_{2}}(T)
since
\mathbf{t}_{\gamma_{2}}=[0,1]^{T}
. Thanks to identity
eq.

119
, which was shown in the proof of
theorem

41
to hold for all
f\in W^{1,p}_{\gamma_{1}}(T)
, the result for
i=1
follows from
lemma

40
. The case
i=2
can be reduced to the case
i=1
using similar arguments as in the proof of
theorem

41
using the mapping
\mathfrak{F}_{1}

eq.

95
.

Appendix B
Equivalent Boundary Norm

We begin with a result that states necessary and sufficient conditions for a function defined on two faces
\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}\subset\partial K
to belong to
W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
.

Lemma 43
.

Let
0\leq s<1
,
1<p<\infty
, and
1\leq i<j\leq 4
. Then,
f\in L^{p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
satisfies
f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
if and only if

(i)

f_{i}\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i})
and
f_{i}\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i})
;

(ii)

if
s>1/p
, then
f_{i}|_{\gamma_{ij}}=f_{j}|_{\gamma_{ij}}
; and

(iii)

if
s=1/p
, then
I_{ij}^{p}(f_{i},f_{j})<\infty
,

where
\mathcal{I}_{ij}^{p}(\cdot,\cdot)
is defined in
eq.

2
. Additionally,

\displaystyle\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}\approx_{s,p}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}:=\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}}^{p}+\|f_{j}\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{j}}^{p}+\begin{cases}\mathcal{I}_{ij}^{p}(f_{i},f_{j})&\text{if }sp=1,\\
0&\text{otherwise}.\end{cases}

Proof.

Let
0\leq s<1
,
1<p<\infty
, and
1\leq i<j\leq 4
be given.

Step 1:
f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})\implies\text{(i-iii)}
.
Assume first that
f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
. Condition (i) follows from the definition of the norms, and in particular,
\|f_{i}\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}}^{p}+\|f_{j}\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{j}}^{p}\leq\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}
. If
s>1/p
, then the trace theorem shows that
f
has a well-defined trace on
\gamma_{ij}
, and so (ii) holds.

We now show that condition (iii) is satisfied. There holds

\displaystyle|\bm{x}-\bm{y}|^{2}\approx([\bm{F}_{ij}^{-1}(\bm{x})-\bm{F}_{ji}^{-1}(\bm{y})]\cdot\mathbf{e}_{1})^{2}+([\bm{F}_{ij}^{-1}(\bm{x})+\bm{F}_{ji}^{-1}(\bm{y})]\cdot\mathbf{e}_{2})^{2}

for all
(\bm{x},\bm{y})\in\Gamma_{i}\times\Gamma_{j}
, where
\bm{F}_{ij}:T\to\Gamma_{i}
and
\bm{F}_{ji}:T\to\Gamma_{j}
are defined in
eq.

1
, and so

\displaystyle\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\approx_{s,p}\int_{\Gamma_{i}}\int_{\Gamma_{j}}\frac{|f_{i}(\bm{x})-f_{j}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}

(121)

\displaystyle<\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p},

where
\bm{x}_{r}:=(x_{1},-x_{2})
.
Now let
s=1/p
. Applying the triangle inequality in conjunction with the above inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{p}\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}.

Assume, for the moment, that the following holds.

\displaystyle A(\bm{x}):=\int_{T}\frac{x_{2}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\gtrsim 1\qquad\forall\bm{x}\in T.

(122)

Then, we obtain the following bound for
I_{ij}^{p}(f_{i},f_{j})
defined in
eq.

2
:

\displaystyle I_{ij}^{p}(f_{i},f_{j})\lesssim\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\lesssim_{p}\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma}^{p}.

Thus,
f
satisfies (iii) and we have shown that for all
f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
there holds

\displaystyle\|f\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}\gtrsim_{s,p}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}.

(123)

We now turn to the proof of
eq.

122
. First note that
A
is well-defined on the half plane
\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}=\{\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{2}:x_{2}>0\}
and is a continuous function with
A(\bm{x})>0
for
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}
. Moreover, a tedious calculation (in Step 3) shows that

\displaystyle A(\bm{x})=\frac{x_{1}(x_{2}^{2}+(1-x_{1})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}+(1-x_{1}+x_{2})(x_{1}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}-x_{2}((1+x_{2})^{2}+x_{1}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{x_{1}(1-x_{1}+x_{2})},

and so
\lim_{x_{2}\to 0^{+}}A(\bm{x})=2
for all
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}_{+}^{2}
. Inequality
eq.

122
now follows.

Step 2:
\text{(i-iii)}\implies f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
.
Now assume that
f\in L^{p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
satisfies (i-iii). Thanks to the triangle inequality, there holds

\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\\
\lesssim_{s,p}\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\\
\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}.

(124)

We appeal to
eq.

121
to bound the second term:

\displaystyle\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\lesssim_{s,p}\|f_{j}\|_{s,p,\Gamma_{j}}^{p}.

For the first term, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{T}\frac{\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\leq\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}\setminus B(\bm{x}_{r},x_{2})}\frac{\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}=2\pi\int_{x_{2}}^{\infty}\frac{\,\mathrm{d}{\rho}}{\rho^{sp+1}}=\frac{2\pi}{sp}x_{2}^{-sp},

for all
\bm{x}\in T
, and so

\displaystyle\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\lesssim_{s,p}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{x_{2}^{sp}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}.

Thanks to conditions (ii)-(iii), the function
g=f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}
belongs to
W_{\gamma_{2}}^{s,p}(T)
and applying
eq.

117
and the triangle inequality gives

\displaystyle\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{x})|^{p}}{x_{2}^{sp}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\lesssim_{s,p}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}

and so

\displaystyle\int_{T}\int_{T}\frac{|f_{i}\circ\bm{F}_{ij}(\bm{x})-f_{j}\circ\bm{F}_{ji}(\bm{y})|^{p}}{|\bm{x}_{r}-\bm{y}|^{sp+2}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{x}}\lesssim_{s,p}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|f\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{s,p,\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}^{p}.

Then, the reverse inequality of
eq.

123
immediately follows from
eq.

121
, and so
f\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
and the result follows.

Step 3: Computing
A(\bm{x})
.
First note that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{1-y_{2}}\frac{\,\mathrm{d}{y_{1}}}{[(y_{1}-x_{1})^{2}+z^{2}]^{\frac{3}{2}}}=\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\frac{1-y_{2}-x_{1}}{((1-y_{2}-x_{1})^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{x_{1}}{(x_{1}^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right),

and so

\displaystyle\int_{T}\frac{\,\mathrm{d}{\bm{y}}}{[(x_{1}-y_{1})^{2}+(x_{2}+y_{2})^{2}]^{\frac{3}{2}}}

\displaystyle\qquad=\int_{0}^{1}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left[\frac{1-y_{2}-x_{1}}{((1-y_{2}-x_{1})^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{x_{1}}{(x_{1}^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right]_{z=x_{2}+y_{2}}\,\mathrm{d}{y_{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\kern-12.87743pt\mathrel{\mathop{=}\limits^{z=x_{2}+y_{2}}}\int_{x_{2}}^{1+x_{2}}\frac{1}{z^{2}}\left(\frac{1+x_{2}-x_{1}-z}{((1+x_{2}-x_{1}-z)^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}+\frac{x_{1}}{(x_{1}^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\right)\,\mathrm{d}{z}.

Now note that for any
a\in\mathbb{R}
, there holds

\displaystyle\int_{x_{2}}^{1+x_{2}}\frac{a-z}{z^{2}((a-z)^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\mathrm{d}{z}=\frac{(x_{2}^{2}+(a-x_{2})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{ax_{2}}-\frac{((1+x_{2})^{2}+(a-1-x_{2})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{a(1+x_{2})},

and

\displaystyle\int_{x_{2}}^{1+x_{2}}\frac{x_{1}}{z^{2}(x_{1}^{2}+z^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}\,\mathrm{d}{z}=\frac{(x_{1}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{x_{1}x_{2}}-\frac{(x_{1}^{2}+(1+x_{2})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{x_{1}(1+x_{2})}.

Consequently, there holds

\displaystyle A(\bm{x})

\displaystyle=\frac{(x_{2}^{2}+(1-x_{1})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(1-x_{1}+x_{2})}+\frac{(x_{1}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{x_{1}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-x_{2}((1+x_{2})^{2}+x_{1}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\frac{1}{(1+x_{2}-x_{1})(1+x_{2})}+\frac{1}{x_{1}(1+x_{2})}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{x_{1}(x_{2}^{2}+(1-x_{1})^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}+(1-x_{1}+x_{2})(x_{1}^{2}+x_{2}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}-x_{2}((1+x_{2})^{2}+x_{1}^{2})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{x_{1}(1-x_{1}+x_{2})}.

On noting that
f\in W^{s,p}(\partial K)
if and only if
f\in L^{p}(\partial K)
and
f|_{\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j}}\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i}\cup\Gamma_{j})
for all
1\leq i<j\leq 4
, the following result is an immediate consequence of
lemma

43
.

Corollary 44
.

Let
0\leq s<1
and
1<p<\infty
. Then,
f\in L^{p}(\partial K)
satisfies
f\in W^{s,p}(\partial K)
if and only if

(i)

f_{i}\in W^{s,p}(\Gamma_{i})
for
1\leq i\leq 4
;

(ii)

if
s>1/p
, then
f_{i}|_{\gamma_{ij}}=f_{j}|_{\gamma_{ij}}
for all
1\leq i<j\leq 4
; and

(iii)

if
s=1/p
, then
I_{ij}^{p}(f_{i},f_{j})<\infty
for all
1\leq i<j\leq 4
.

Additionally,
eq.

3
holds.

Appendix C
Partial fractions decomposition

Lemma 45
.

For all
\beta\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
with
|\beta|\geq 2
, there holds

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}}}=\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\alpha\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}\\
\alpha_{j}\leq\beta_{j}\\
|\alpha|\geq 2\end{subarray}}\left(\frac{c_{\alpha,1}}{\omega_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\omega_{2}^{\alpha_{2}}}+\frac{c_{\alpha,2}}{\omega_{1}^{\alpha_{1}}\omega_{3}^{\alpha_{3}}}+\frac{c_{\alpha,3}}{\omega_{2}^{\alpha_{2}}\omega_{3}^{\alpha_{3}}}\right)\qquad\text{in }T,

(125)

where
\{c_{\alpha,j}\}
are suitable positive constants.

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
|\beta|
. The case
|\beta|=2
is trivially true. Assume that
eq.

125
holds for all
\beta\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
with
|\beta|=r\geq 2
. Let
\beta\in\mathbb{N}_{0}^{3}
with
|\beta|=r+1
. If
\beta_{j}=0
for some
j\in\{1,2,3\}
, then
eq.

125
is trivially true, so assume that
\beta_{j}>0
for
1\leq j\leq 3
. Then,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}\cdot\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}-1}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}-1}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}-1}}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\omega_{1}\omega_{2}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{1}\omega_{3}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{2}\omega_{3}}\right)\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}-1}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}-1}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}-1}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}-1}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}-1}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}}}+\frac{1}{\omega_{1}^{\beta_{1}-1}\omega_{2}^{\beta_{2}}\omega_{3}^{\beta_{3}}}.

By assumption, each of the three terms above is of the form
eq.

125
, which completes the proof.
